&lt;project_context>
# 项目: struct_self_consist_ie

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: autodl-5090
- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account="root"）
- SSH 连接命令: ssh autodl-5090  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie

## ⚠️ 活跃约束（必须遵守）

- **C001**: 论文 tex 文件只能修改 docs/paper/sections/ 下的编译版本，禁止直接修改 artifacts/_project/paper/ 下的 tex 文件（原因：两次出现 Worker 修改 artifacts/ 旧版本而非 docs/ 编译版本的系统性错误（patch-narrative worker + patch-experiments worker）。artifacts/_project/paper/ 是旧版残留，不参与编译。）


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：检查 14B 训练是否成功完成

### 背景
exp_qwen25_14b_fewnerd 的训练进程在 autodl-5090-c 上已退出，cron 无法自动判断成功/失败。需要检查日志确认。

### 步骤

1. SSH 到 autodl-5090-c

2. 检查训练日志最后 50 行：
```bash
tail -50 /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/logs/train_qwen25_14b_fewnerd_s42.log
```

3. 确认关键信息：
   - 是否显示 "Training completed" 或类似成功标志？
   - 最终 loss 是多少？
   - 总训练步数是否达到预期？
   - 是否有 error/exception/OOM？

4. 检查最终 checkpoint 是否存在：
```bash
ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/checkpoints/ | grep 14b
# 找到最终 checkpoint 目录
find /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/checkpoints/ -name "*14b*" -type d | head -10
```

5. 检查 checkpoint 完整性（如果存在）：
```bash
# 检查文件大小和数量
du -sh /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/checkpoints/*14b*/
ls /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/checkpoints/*14b*/ | wc -l
```

6. 根据检查结果，调用 update_registry：
   - 如果训练成功：update_registry(exp_id="exp_qwen25_14b_fewnerd", field="conclusion", value="success") + 设置 checkpoint_path 和 log_path
   - 如果训练失败：update_registry(exp_id="exp_qwen25_14b_fewnerd", field="conclusion", value="failed") + 设置 failure_reason

7. 调用 delete_cron("exp_qwen25_14b_fewnerd") 清除监控

### 服务器
- SSH: autodl-5090-c
- 项目路径: /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie
- Python 环境: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`

### 重要
- 如果训练成功，记录最终 checkpoint 路径 — 后续需要用于 N∈{1,2,4,8,16} inference
- 如果 checkpoint 已 merge 过（之前有 ckpt36000 early merge），确认最终 checkpoint 是否也需要 merge

Most of the +4.37pp ΔF1 vs Qwen greedy comes from LLaMA being stronger (greedy 0.784 vs 0.748), but the +0.42pp vs pure LLaMA is genuine cross-model diversity gain. Well-separated temperature mixing (T=0.7+T=1.0) achieves Pareto optimality for both uniform and VC construction, while VC-weighted construction flattens the trade-off slope, reducing the diversity cost of quality.\", \"metrics\": {\"dataset\": \"SciERC\", \"configs\": {\"t07\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.777, \"uniform_delta\": 1.81, \"vc_delta\": 1.17, \"pareto\": \"uniform only\"}, \"t08\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.819, \"uniform_delta\": 1.25, \"vc_delta\": 1.68, \"pareto\": \"VC only\"}, \"mix_07_10\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.83, \"uniform_delta\": 1.43, \"vc_delta\": 1.67, \"pareto\": \"both\"}, \"t10\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.877, \"uniform_delta\": 0.75, \"vc_delta\": 1.21, \"pareto\": \"both (highest diversity)\"}, \"mix_08_10\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.789, \"uniform_delta\": -0.17, \"vc_delta\": 0.47, \"pareto\": \"dominated\"}}, \"vc_slope_reduction\": \"~50% flatter than uniform\", \"key_insight\": \"well-separated T mixing dual Pareto optimal; close T mixing dominated\"}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"A4 Pareto diversity-quality analysis on SciERC. Mix T=0.7+T=1.0 is dual-Pareto optimal (uniform +1.43pp, VC +1.67pp). VC construction flattens trade-off slope ~50% vs uniform — increasing diversity costs less quality. Mix T=0.8+T=1.0 is dominated (close T → higher degeneracy, no construction gain). Confirms DGEC heterogeneous-T finding: ΔT matters, ΔT=0.3 beneficial, ΔT=0.2 harmful. Pareto plot artifact registered.\"}, \"exp_adaptive_sampling_budget\": {\"status\": \"PREPARING\", \"retry_count\": 0, \"created_at\": \"2026-05-20T09:29:22.761Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-20T09:29:41.158Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Confidence-aware adaptive sampling budget allocation (fewer samples for high-confidence instances, full N=8 for low-confidence) can recover nearly all SC F1 gains while saving 40-50% of inference cost, because high-confidence instances already have low diversity and gain nothing from additional samples.\", \"metrics\": {\"model\": \"Qwen3-8B FT\", \"fewnerd\": {\"n_instances\": 37648, \"greedy_f1\": 0.7884, \"uniform_n8_f1\": 0.7819, \"best_adaptive\": \"h1_l8\", \"best_adaptive_f1\": 0.7849, \"best_delta_vs_u8\": \"+0.30pp\", \"sample_savings\": \"43.8%\", \"note\": \"Greedy > U8; adaptive h1/l8 best: greedy for high-conf, N=8 SC for low-conf\"}, \"scierc\": {\"n_instances\": 551, \"greedy_f1\": 0.6592, \"uniform_n8_f1\": 0.6712, \"best_adaptive\": \"top67%_h2_l8\", \"best_adaptive_f1\": 0.6707, \"best_delta_vs_u8\": \"-0.05pp\", \"sample_savings\": \"50.2%\", \"note\": \"U8 > Greedy; top67% h2/l8 saves 50% with negligible loss\"}}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"B7 adaptive sampling budget analysis on Qwen3-8B FT. Key findings: (1) FewNERD: greedy already beats uniform N=8 SC (+0.65pp), adaptive h1/l8 is optimal (+0.30pp vs U8, saves 43.8% samples); (2) SciERC: U8 beats greedy (+1.20pp), top67% h2/l8 saves 50% with only -0.05pp loss. Practical implication: confidence-gated sampling can halve inference cost.
